Now, how to generate initial p with sum 1:
```
def make_initial_patterns(n, rng):
    pats=[]
    x=np.arange(n).astype(float)
    # inverse sqrt offsets
    for off in [0.5,1.0]: p=(x+off)**-0.5; p=p/sum
    # power alpha beta
    for alpha in [0.45,0.48,0.5,0.52,0.55]:
       for beta in [0.0,0.1,0.25,0.5]:
          p=(x+1)**(-alpha) * (n+1-x)**(-beta) # if beta>0, at x=n-1:1^-beta=1
          # maybe use (n+1-x) for beta
          p=p/sum
    # finite endpoints: p = invsqrt + c*endpoint? normalize
    for c in [0.1,0.5,1]: p = (x+1)^-0.5 + c*((n+1-x)^-0.5); normalize
    # random low pass: p = exp(random/noise smoothed)
    for seed in range(...):
       y = rng.normal(0,0.3,n); smooth via FFT filtering; p = exp(y - max)/sum
    # if height_sequence_1 exists, resample
```

- `fft_convolve(a, b=None)`: If b None, b=a. Return real array.
```
def fft_convolve(a, b=None):
    if b is None: b=a
    m=len(a); l=len(b); n=m+l-1; N=1
    while N<n: N*=2
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb, N)
    return c[:n]
```
But for p and w_rev length n and 2n-1, N next pow of 3n? e.g., n=4096, length=4096+8191-1=12286 -> N=16384. Good. Could optimize by allowing N parameter. Use float64. For p*p, length 2n-1; N=next pow 2n-1. For p,w length 3n-2; N separate. Could create helper `fft_convolve(a,b,N=None)`.

Implement `solve_lp_weights(p, M, n, time_limit)`:
```
def solve_lp_weights(p, M, timeout=10):
    import scipy.optimize as opt
    L=2*n-1
    B = np.zeros((n, L), dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(n):
        B[i, i:i+n] = p
    c = np.ones(L)
    A_ub = -B
    b_ub = -np.ones(n)
    res = opt.linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'time_limit': timeout, 'presolve': True})
    if res.status in (0,3?):
       y=res.x
       y=np.clip(y,0,None)
       if y.sum()>0: return y/y.sum()
    return None
```
But memory of `B` and `A_ub=-B` doubles. We can modify in-place: `A_ub = B *= -1`; but need B? No. Use `B = np.zeros; fill; B *= -1; A_ub=B`. Good. For n=2048, 67MB for B; other arrays <50; total maybe 150MB. Could be okay but unknown. Use n=1024 default. We can choose `lp_n = min(n, 1024)`. Resampling: To initialize larger n from smaller best, use:
```
def resample_sequence(p, new_n):
    n=len(p)
    if new_n==n: return p
    old_x=(np.arange(n)-0.5)/n
    new_x=(np.arange(new_n)-0.5)/new_n
    # Use log interpolation for positive; if zeros, linear.
    logs=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
    new_logs=np.interp(new_x, old_x, logs)
    q=np.exp(new_logs - new_logs.max())
    return q/q.sum()
```
But resampling a discrete optimized sequence may smooth finite structure; still good.

Detailed code skeleton:
```
import numpy as np
import time

def next_pow2(m):
    N=1
    while N<m: N*=2
    return N

def fft_convolve(a,b,N=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64); b=np.asarray(b,dtype=np.float64)
    if b is a: ...
    n=a.size; m=b.size; L=n+m-1
    if N is None: N=next_pow2(L)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,N)
    return c[:L]
```
Careful `if b is a` works after asarray? If b=None set b=a. Then asarray. Good.

Mirror refine with LP:
```
def refine_sequence(p0, rng, deadline, use_lp=False, max_lp=3, lp_timeout=20):
    n=p0.size
    p=normalize(p0,1.0)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score_internal(p)
    z=np.log(p+1e-300); z-=np.mean(z)
    eta=0.05
    temp_idx=0
    temps=[0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0]
    stale=0
    last_lp=time.monotonic()
    lp_count=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
        # mirror step
        c=fft_convolve(p,p)
        maxc=float(np.max(c))
        score=float(2*n*maxc)
        if score < best_score - 1e-12: update
        if maxc <=0: break
        temp = temps[temp_idx % len]; temp_idx+=1
        x=(c-maxc)/(maxc*temp)
        x-=x.max(); w=np.exp(x); w/=w.sum()
        # If using LP weights maybe; but skip
        conv_pw=fft_convolve(p, w[::-1])
        g=conv_pw[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
        grad = g - float(np.dot(p,g))
        gn=np.linalg.norm(grad)
        if not finite or gn==0: random
        # Try several eta maybe [eta, 0.5eta]
        improved_this=False
        for et in (eta, 0.5*eta, 0.2*eta):
            z_new = z - et*grad
            z_new -= np.mean(z_new)
            p_new = softmax(z_new)
            sc=score_internal(p_new)
            if finite and sc < score - 1e-12:
                # accept
                p=p_new; z=z_new; best? update if sc best
                eta = min(et*1.2, 1.0)
                improved_this=True
                stale=0
                break
        if not improved_this:
            eta *=0.8
            stale +=1
            # random perturbation
            if stale % 20 ==0:
                for scale in [0.05,0.2,0.5]:
                   z_new = z + rng.normal(0,scale,n)*lowpass?; normalize
                   sc=score_internal(softmax(z_new))
                   if sc < best_score: accept update stale=0
        # LP step occasionally
        if use_lp and time.monotonic()-last_lp > 60 and lp_count < max_lp and time_left>lp_timeout+60:
           dir = lp_direction(p, maxc, timeout=lp_timeout)
           lp_count+=1; last_lp=time.monotonic()
           if dir is not None:
              for t in [0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0]:
                 p_mix=normalize((1-t)*p + t*dir,1.0)
                 sc=score_internal(p_mix)
                 if sc < best_score: update p? keep current? Best candidate set to p_mix but may not improve current; we can set p=p_mix if sc<score (current) else keep? To explore, set p to best_mix even if not below current? Could explore outside basin. Maybe set p to best_mix if sc < best_score (global) -> update. If not, don't.
```

Now, LP direction. Let's implement carefully.

```
def conv_matrix_p(p):
    n=p.size
    L=2*n-1
    A=np.zeros((L,n), dtype=np.float64)
    # For each column j, fill rows j:j+n with p[:n-j]? Wait derived column j: rows k=j to j+n-1, A[k,j] = p_{k-j}; so full p from 0 to n-1. Example j=0 rows 0..n-1 p; j=1 rows1..n p; yes.
    for j in range(n):
        A[j:j+n, j] = p
    return A
```
Check constraint A@x: (A x)_k = ∑_j p_{k-j} x_j if j≤k≤j+n-1 (i.e., 0≤k-j<n). This equals ∑_{j=0}^{min(k,n-1)} p_{k-j} x_j if k-j≥0. For k<n, j=0..k, p_{k-j}x_j. Convolution p*x at k =∑_{j=0}^k p_{k-j}x_j (if using p first x second) yes. For k≥n, j=k-n+1..n-1, p_{k-j}x_j, matches. Good.

Linprog:
```
def lp_direction(p, timeout=10.0):
    n=p.size
    if n<2: return None
    import scipy.optimize as opt
    A=conv_matrix_p(p)
    c_obj = -np.ones(n)
    # A_x <= 1. A_ub = -A? linprog requires A_ub x <= b_ub.
    A = -A
    b_ub = np.ones(2*n-1)
    try:
       res = opt.linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0.0,None), method='highs', options={'time_limit': int(timeout), 'presolve': True})
    except Exception:
       res=opt.linprog(... options={'presolve': True})
    if res.status in (0,3):
       x=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
       x=np.clip(x,0,None)
       s=x.sum()
       if s>1e-15 and np.isfinite(s): return x/s
    return None
```
But if RHS 1, feasible x may be very large; objective -sum, max sum. HiGHS may have difficulty due scale. Since p sum=1, A columns? We can scale A and b to improve: Multiply constraints by n maybe: A*x ≤ n. Since p~1/n, A entries small; scaling by n makes entries ~1. Use `b_ub = np.full(2*n-1, n)` and A = -A*n? Wait original constraints: (p*x)_k ≤1. Multiply by n: (n p*x)_k ≤ n. A_orig entries p ~1/n, so A_n entries n p ~1. So set `A_mat = -A * n`; `b_ub = n`. Objective unchanged. This is more scaled. But if p has peaks, n p up to n. No problem. Let's do that. Actually if p normalized, n p entries O(1) average. Good.

Could also divide x by something? No.

Detailed `refine_sequence(p0, rng, deadline, use_lp, max_lp, lp_timeout)`:
```
def refine_sequence(p0, rng, deadline, use_lp=False, max_lp=2, lp_timeout=15.0):
    n=p0.size
    p=normalize_nonnegative(p0,1.0)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=internal_score(p)
    z=log(p)
    eta=0.05
    temp_list=[0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0]
    temp_i=0
    fails=0; it=0; last_lp=time.monotonic(); lp_count=0
    consider_candidate(p,best_score)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2:
        it+=1
        # periodic floor
        if it % 256 == 0:
           p = (1-1e-8)*p + 1e-8/n
           p=normalize...
           z=np.log(p+...); z-=mean
        c=fft_convolve(p,p)
        maxc=float(c.max())
        score=2*n*maxc
        if not finite or score>=best_score: # use best as p? p should be best
           p=best_p; z=...
        # mirror candidate loop
        improved=False
        if maxc>0:
           temp=temp_list[temp_i % len(temp_list)]; temp_i += rng.integers(0,2?)
           w=...
           conv_pw=fft_convolve(p, w[::-1])
           g=conv_pw[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
           grad = g - np.dot(p,g)
           # scale grad maybe by 2/n? but eta absorbs.
           # Try eta values
           etas = [eta, 0.5*eta, 2.0*eta]
           # To save, test in ascending? Start with best eta.
           for et in etas:
              z_new = z - et*grad
              z_new -= z_new.mean()
              p_new=softmax(z_new)
              sc=internal_score(p_new)
              if finite and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                  best_score=sc; best_p=p_new.copy(); p=best_p.copy(); z=z_new
                  eta=min(max(et*1.1, 0.01), 1.0)
                  improved=True
                  consider_candidate(p, sc)
                  fails=0
                  break
           if not improved:
              eta *=0.85
              fails +=1
        else:
           fails +=1
        # Random perturbation
        if fails > 30:
           for scale in (0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5):
              dr = rng.normal(0, scale, n)
              # low-pass optional: if rng.random()<0.5: apply block
              if rng.random()<0.3:
                 width ... dr *= block mask
              z_new = z + dr
              z_new -= z_new.mean()
              p_new=softmax(z_new)
              sc=internal_score(p_new)
              if finite and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                  accept; rngeta...
                  fails=0
                  break
           if fails > 60:
              # try low-temperature mirror with random temp or reset z toward best + random
              pass
        # LP occasionally
        if use_lp and lp_count<max_lp and time.monotonic() - last_lp > 45 and deadline - time.monotonic() > lp_timeout + 20:
           dir=lp_direction(p, timeout=lp_timeout)
           lp_count+=1; last_lp=time.monotonic()
           if dir is not None:
              for t in (0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,0.9,1.0):
                  p_mix=normalize((1-t)*p + t*dir,1.0)
                  sc=internal_score(p_mix)
                  if finite and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                      best_score=sc; best_p=p_mix.copy(); z=log(p); eta=0.05; fails=0; consider_candidate
                      break
              # even if no single t improves best, try nearest? no
    return best_p, best_score
```

`consider_candidate(seq, score)`:
```
def consider_candidate(seq, score):
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    if seq.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(seq).all(): return
    seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = seq.sum()
    if s <= 0: return
    # internal score maybe if not provided? We have score; but if not finite use internal
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        score = internal_score(seq / s * 1.0)  # need scale? score invariant but internal expects sum? If seq not normalized, internal handles sum.
    # To keep memory, store normalized to sum 1
    nseq = seq / s
    if np.isfinite(score) and score < _GLOBAL['best_score'] - 1e-12:
        _GLOBAL['best_score'] = score
        _GLOBAL['best_seq'] = nseq.copy()
    # append to top if different enough
    for item in _GLOBAL['top']:
       d = np.linalg.norm(seq - item[0]) / (np.linalg.norm(seq)+1e-30)
       if d < 1e-7: break
    else:
       _GLOBAL['top'].append((nseq.copy(), float(score)))
       _GLOBAL['top'].sort(key=lambda? no lambda. Use custom sort by comparing.
```

```
def lp_direction(p, timeout=15.0):
    n=int(p.size)
    if n < 16: return None
    try:
       import scipy.optimize as sopt
    except Exception:
       return None
    p = np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: return None
    p = p/s
    L=2*n-1
    try:
       A=np.zeros((L,n), dtype=np.float64)
       for j in range(n):
           A[j:j+n, j] = p
       A *= -n  # scaled constraints
       b = np.full(L, float(n))
       c = -np.ones(n)
       res = sopt.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, None), method='highs', options={'time_limit': max(1, int(timeout)), 'presolve': True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance': 1e-8, 'primal_feasibility_tolerance': 1e-8})
       status=res.status
       if status in (0,3) and res.x is not None:
          x=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
          x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
          x=np.clip(x,0.0,None)
          s2=x.sum()
          if s2>1e-12:
             return x/s2
    except Exception:
       pass
    return None
```
But A scaling: constraints (p*x)_k ≤1. Multiply by n: (n p*x)_k ≤ n. A_orig entries p. A = -A_orig * n, b=n. Linprog min -sum x s.t. A_ub x ≤ b => -n p x ≤ n => n p x ≥ -n (always) Wait if A_ub=-A_orig*n, constraint -n (p*x)_k ≤ n => p*x_k ≥ -1/n. That's wrong! Need A_ub x ≤ b with A_ub = A_orig? Standard: A_orig x ≤ 1. We can set A_ub = A_orig, b=1. If scaling, A_ub = A_orig*n, b=n. Not negative. Linprog min with A_ub x ≤ b. So set `A = A_orig; A *= n; b=np.full(L,n)`; no negative. Objective min -sum. Yes! I mistakenly used negative. For linprog, constraints are `A_ub @ x <= b_ub`. Since packing constraints A x ≤1. So A_ub=A. Objective c=-ones. Right.

So `A` nonnegative. No need negative.

5. `log_softmax(z)`:
```
def log_softmax(z):
   z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
   m = np.max(z)
   e = np.exp(z-m)
   s = e.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
       n=z.size; return np.full(n, 1.0/n), np.zeros(n)
   p = e/s
   zp = z - m - np.log(s)
   return p, zp
```
Returning zp can be log p; but due underflow, p may zeros. We can later floor.

def floor_normalize(p):
   n=p.size; f=1e-12
   p=p.copy(); p[np.where(p<f)] = f
   p/=p.sum(); return p
```
But if p size large, floor 1e-12 sum floor 4e-9 for n=4096, negligible.

def make_weights(c, temp):
    if temp <=0: ...
    maxc=max(c)
    if maxc<=0: return np.full(c.size,1/c.size)
    x=(c-maxc)/(maxc*temp)
    x-=x.max()
    w=np.exp(x)
    s=w.sum(); if s<=0: uniform
    return w/s
```

def random_perturb_z(z, rng, scale=0.1):
   n=z.size
   if rng.random() < 0.4:
      width=max(1, int(rng.integers(max(1,n//50), max(2,n//5))))
      start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
      dr=np.zeros(n)
      dr[start:start+width]=rng.normal(0,scale,width)
   else:
      dr=make_lowpass(z,rng,scale)

```
def refine_sequence(p0, rng, deadline, use_lp=False, max_lp=3, lp_timeout=15.0):
    n=p0.size
    p=normalize_sequence(p0,1.0)
    p=floor_normalize(p,1e-14)
    best_score=internal_score(p)
    best_p=p.copy()
    cur_p=p.copy(); cur_score=best_score
    z=np.log(cur_p + 1e-300); z -= np.mean(z)
    eta=0.05
    temp_list=(0.03,0.08,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0)
    temp_i=0
    fails=0
    it=0
    last_best=time.monotonic()
    last_lp=time.monotonic()
    lp_count=0
    consider_candidate(cur_p, best_score)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
       it += 1
       time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
       if time_left <= 2: break
       # If abandoned for too long, reset
       if time.monotonic() - last_best > 60.0 and it > 100:
           cur_p=best_p.copy(); cur_score=best_score; z=np.log(cur_p+1e-300); z-=np.mean(z); eta=0.05; fails=0
       # periodic floor
       if it % 256 == 0:
           for arr in (cur_p, best_p): ...? Can't modify list. Do cur and best separately.
           cur_p = floor_normalize(cur_p, 1e-14)
           best_p = floor_normalize(best_p, 1e-14)
           cur_score = internal_score(cur_p)? maybe expensive; if cur_score != best? We can skip.
           z = np.log(cur_p+1e-300); z-=np.mean(z)
       c = fft_convolve(cur_p, cur_p)
       maxc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
       s = float(cur_p.sum())
       cur_score = float(2*n*maxc/(s*s)) if s>0 else np.inf
       if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
           cur_p=best_p.copy(); ...
           continue
       if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score = cur_score; best_p=cur_p.copy(); last_best=time.monotonic(); consider_candidate(best_p, best_score); fails=0
       improved=False
       if maxc > 0:
           temp = temp_list[temp_i % len(temp_list)]
           temp_i = int((temp_i + 1 + rng.integers(0,2))) % len(temp_list)
           w = make_weights(c, temp)
           g = gradient_from_weights(cur_p, w)
           grad = g - float(np.dot(cur_p, g))
           gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
           if np.isfinite(gnorm) and gnorm > 1e-15:
               # scale eta relative to进气格栅? Maybe divide by n? grad mean? Let's estimate.
               # For uniform p, c triangular. grad values maybe O(1). eta=0.05 might be okay.
               # Try eta values; start with current.
               # To save evaluations, try at most 3; break.
               for et in (eta, 0.5*eta, 2.0*eta):
                   if et <= 0: continue
                   z_new = z - et*grad
                   # Optionally center z_new
                   z_new = z_new - np.mean(z_new)
                   p_new, z_step = log_softmax(z_new)
                   p_new = floor_normalize(p_new, 1e-14)
                   p_new = normalize_sequence(p_new, 1.0) # floornormalize already but okay
                   sc = internal_score(p_new)
                   if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
                       # accept locally
                       z = np.log(p_new + 1e-300); z -= np.mean(z)
                       cur_p = p_new
                       cur_score = sc
                       if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                           best_score = sc
                           best_p = p_new.copy()
                           last_best = time.monotonic()
                           consider_candidate(best_p, best_score)
                       improved=True
                       eta = min(max(et*1.15, 0.005), 1.5)
                       fails=0
                       break
       if not improved:
           eta = max(eta*0.85, 0.001)
           fails += 1
           # Random exploration if fails high
           if fails > 20:
               tried = False
               for scale in (0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0):
                   z_r = random_perturb_z(z, rng, scale)
                   z_r = z_r - np.mean(z_r)
                   p_r, _ = log_softmax(z_r)
                   p_r = floor_normalize(p_r, 1e-14)
                   sc_r = internal_score(p_r)
                   # Accept if improves current; also sometimes if improves best even if cur worse? cur_score maybe.
                   if np.isfinite(sc_r) and sc_r < cur_score - 1e-12:
                       z = np.log(p_r+1e-300); z-=np.mean(z)
                       cur_p=p_r; cur_score=sc_r
                       if sc_r < best_score - 1e-12:
                           best_score=sc_r; best_p=p_r.copy(); last_best=...; consider_candidate
                       fails=0
                       tried=True
                       break
                   # If much worse but less than best by 0.01? no
               if not tried and fails > 80:
                   # reset to best and random
                   cur_p=best_p.copy(); cur_score=best_score; z=...; fails=30
       # LP step
       if use_lp and lp_count < max_lp and time.monotonic() - last_lp > 40.0 and time_left > lp_timeout + 30:
           # maybe use best_p if cur_score worse? Use best_p for LP to get direction
           base_for_lp = best_p if best_score < cur_score else cur_p
           dir = lp_direction(base_for_lp, timeout=lp_timeout)
           lp_count += 1
           last_lp = time.monotonic()
           if dir is not None:
               best_mix_score = np.inf
               best_mix_p = None
               for t in (0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7,0.9,1.0):
                   p_m = normalize_sequence((1.0-t)*base_for_lp + t*dir, 1.0)
                   p_m = floor_normalize(p_m, 1e-14)
                   sc_m = internal_score(p_m)
                   if np.isfinite(sc_m) and sc_m < best_mix_score:
                       best_mix_score = sc_m; best_mix_p = p_m
               if best_mix_p is not None:
                   # Accept if beats current or best
                   if best_mix_score < cur_score - 1e-12 or best_mix_score < best_score - 1e-12 or (best_mix_score < best_score + 0.02 and rng.random() < 0.5):
                       cur_p = best_mix_p.copy(); cur_score = best_mix_score
                       z = np.log(cur_p+1e-300); z-=np.mean(z)
                       eta = 0.05; fails=0
                       if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                           best_score = cur_score; best_p=cur_p.copy(); last_best=...; consider_candidate
                       else:
                           # if it beats best? handled
                           pass
                   else:
                       # late? 
                       pass
    return best_p, best_score
```

```
def random_perturb_z(z, rng, scale):
   n=len(z)
   if rng.random()<0.35:
      width=...; dr=zeros; dr[start:stop]=rng.normal(0,scale,width)
   else:
      dr=rng.normal(0,scale,n)
      # low-pass by moving average repeated
      kernel=np.ones(3)/3
      dr=np.convolve(dr,kernel,mode='same')
      dr=np.convolve(dr,kernel,mode='same')
   return z+dr
```
This avoids FFT.

Now, `random_perturb_z`: If block width >n, adjust.
```
def random_perturb_z(z, rng, scale):
    n = z.size
    if n == 0: return z
    if rng.random() < 0.35:
       max_width = max(2, min(n, n//4 + 1))
       min_width = max(1, min(2, max_width))
       if max_width > 1:
          width = int(rng.integers(min_width, max_width+1))
       else: width=1
       start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
       dr=np.zeros(n)
       dr[start:start+width]=rng.normal(0.0,scale,width)
    else:
       dr=rng.normal(0.0,scale,n)
       # low-pass twice
       kern=np.ones(3,dtype=np.float64)/3.0
       dr=np.convolve(dr,kern,mode='same')
       dr=np.convolve(dr,kern,mode='same')
    return z + dr
```
No lambda.

`floor_normalize`:
```
def floor_normalize(x, floor=1e-14):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x=np.clip(x, 0.0, None)
    if floor > 0:
       x=np.maximum(x, floor)
    s=x.sum()
    if s <= 0:
       return np.ones_like(x)/max(1,x.size)
    x = x * (1.0/s)
    return x
```
This normalizes to sum1. If target sum other? We always sum1. For general target, can later scale. Use in final if needed? okay.

`normalize_sequence` target_sum:
```
def normalize_sequence(x, target_sum=1.0):
   x=np.asarray... clip 0..1000, finite
   s=x.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.ones_like(x)*target_sum/x.size
   x=x*target_sum/s
   return x
```
If x huge, target sum1; okay.

```
def consider_candidate(seq, score):
    seq=np.asarray(seq,dtype=np.float64)
    if seq.size==0: return
    seq=np.where(np.isfinite(seq),seq,0.0); seq=np.clip(seq,0.0,1000.0)
    s=seq.sum()
    if s<=0: return
    nseq=seq/s
    if not np.isfinite(score): score=internal_score(nseq)
    if not np.isfinite(score): return
    # update best
    if score < _GLOBAL['best_score'] - 1e-12:
       _GLOBAL['best_score']=float(score)
       _GLOBAL['best_seq']=nseq.copy()
    # add if top cap
    for entry in _GLOBAL['top']:
       # compare relative diff
       diff = np.linalg.norm(nseq - entry[0])
       if diff < 1e-8:
          break
    else:
       if len(_GLOBAL['top']) >= 60:
          # remove worst (max score)
          worst=0
          for i in range(1,len(_GLOBAL['top'])):
             if _GLOBAL['top'][i][1] > _GLOBAL['top'][worst][1]: worst=i
          if score >= _GLOBAL['top'][worst][1]:
             return
          del _GLOBAL['top'][worst]
       _GLOBAL['top'].append((nseq.copy(), float(score)))
```
No lambda. If top empty, for loop skipped, else executes? Python `for...else` executes if no break. Good. But if top empty, else executes, append. Need be careful: The else of for runs after loop if not broken. Good.

def select_best_initial(n, rng, ref_seq):
   patterns=initial_patterns(n, rng, ref_seq)
   best_p=None; best_s=np.inf
   for p in patterns:
       p=floor_normalize(p,1e-14)
       sc=internal_score(p)
       if sc<best_s: best_p=p; best_s=sc
   if best_p is None:
       best_p=floor_normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),1e-14)
   return best_p
```

```
def choose_final_candidates(top, max_evals, max_large_evals):
    candidates=[]
    # include global best first
    best_seq = _GLOBAL['best_seq']
    if best_seq is not None: candidates.append(best_seq)
    # sort top by internal score manually
    ordered=[]
    for entry in top:
       score=entry[1]; p=entry[0]
       # insert
       pos=len(ordered)
       for i in range(len(ordered)):
          if score < ordered[i][1]: pos=i; break
       ordered.insert(pos, entry)
       if len(ordered)>50: ordered.pop()
    for entry in ordered:
       if len(candidates) >= max_evals: break
       # avoid duplicate with existing candidates: norm diff
       dup=False
       for c in candidates:
          if np.linalg.norm(entry[0]-c) < 1e-8: dup=True; break
       if not dup: candidates.append(entry[0])
    return candidates
```

import numpy as np
import time

_GLOBAL = {'best_score': np.inf, 'best_seq': None, 'top': []}

def next_pow2(m):
    N=1
    while N < m:
        N <<= 1
    return N
...

`fft_convolve`:
```
def fft_convolve(a, b=None):
    if b is None:
        b = a
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    m = b.size
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
       return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = n + m - 1
    N = next_pow2(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, N)
    return c[:L]
```
If a,b real nonnegative, okay. Could set small negative to 0? Do in callers.

`internal_score`:
```
def internal_score(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size==0: return np.inf
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x=np.clip(x,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15: return np.inf
    c=fft_convolve(x,x)
    c=np.where(c>0.0, c, 0.0)
    maxc=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(maxc): return np.inf
    return float(2.0*int(x.size)*maxc/(s*s))
```
For p sum1, okay.

`floor_normalize` as above.

`log_softmax`:
```
def log_softmax(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size==0: return np.array([]), z
    m=float(np.max(z))
    e=np.exp(z-m)
    s=e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
       n=z.size
       return (np.ones(n)/n), (np.zeros(n))
    p=e/s
    out_z = z - m - np.log(s)
    return p, out_z
```
If p has zeros from underflow, p nonnegative; floor later.

`make_weights`:
```
def make_weights(c, temp):
    c=np.asarray(c,dtype=np.float64)
    c=np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c=np.clip(c,0.0,None)
    n=c.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    maxc=float(np.max(c))
    if maxc <= 0 or not np.isfinite(maxc) or temp <=0:
       return np.full(n, 1.0/n)
    x=(c-maxc)/(maxc*temp)
    x=x-np.max(x)
    w=np.exp(x)
    s=w.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0:
       return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    return w/s
```
If temp very small, underflow all except max? If all low underflow to 0, s=0 => uniform. Good.

`gradient_from_weights`:
```
def gradient_from_weights(p, w):
    n=p.size
    conv=fft_convolve(p, w[::-1])
    return conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```
If w size not 2n-1? only called with that. Could assert not.

Code:
```
def initial_patterns(n, rng, ref_seq=None):
    pats=[]
    x=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p=np.ones(n); pats.append(p)
    for off in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
       pats.append(np.power(x+off, -0.5))
    for alpha in (0.45,0.48,0.5,0.52,0.55,0.6):
       left=np.power(x+1.0, -alpha)
       for beta in (0.0,0.2,0.5,0.8):
          if beta == 0.0:
             p=left
          else:
             right=np.maximum(n - x, 0.5)
             p=left*np.power(right, -beta)
          pats.append(p)
    for w in (0.0,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0):
       p=np.power(x+0.5, -0.5) + w*np.power(n+0.5-x, -0.5)
       pats.append(p)
    # smooth random
    for _ in range(4):
       y=rng.normal(0.0,0.5,n)
       ker=np.ones(3,dtype=np.float64)/3.0
       y=np.convolve(y,ker,mode='same')
       y=np.convolve(y,ker,mode='same')
       y-=y.mean()
       p=np.exp(y - np.max(y))
       pats.append(p)
    # shuffled inverse sqrt
    inv=np.power(x+0.5, -0.5)
    for _ in range(3):
       pats.append(rng.permutation(inv))
    if ref_seq is not None:
       ref=np.asarray(ref_seq,dtype=np.float64)
       if ref.size>0 and np.isfinite(ref).all() and ref.sum()>0:
          ref=ref/ref.sum()
          pats.append(resample_sequence(ref,n))
          if ref.size==n: pats.append(ref.copy())
    return pats
```
If n=1, ranges? Not used. For alpha betas, right = n - x: at x=n-1, right=1; at x=0, n. If beta=0.8, left endpoint? okay. If n small, right min 1.

`resample_sequence`:
```
def resample_sequence(p, new_n):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    n=p.size
    if new_n <=0: return p
    if n == new_n: return p.copy()
    s=p.sum()
    if s <=0: return np.ones(new_n)/new_n
    p=p/s
    old=(np.arange(n)-0.5)/float(n)
    new=(np.arange(new_n)-0.5)/float(new_n)
    eps=1e-300
    logs=np.log(p+eps)
    new_logs=np.interp(new, old, logs)
    q=np.exp(new_logs - np.max(new_logs))
    qsum=q.sum()
    if qsum <=0: q=np.ones(new_n)/new_n
    else: q/=qsum
    return q
```
If p has zeros, log eps. Good.

`lp_direction` as above. Need import scipy inside to avoid failure? We can import at top? Rules allow. But if scipy not available? It is. Use inside to catch.

```
def lp_direction(p, timeout=15.0):
    n=int(p.size)
    if n < 16 or timeout <=0: return None
    try:
       import scipy.optimize as sopt
    except Exception:
       return None
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    # use floor for LP to avoid degeneracy
    p = floor_normalize(p, 1e-8)
    L=2*n-1
    try:
       A=np.zeros((L,n), dtype=np.float64)
       for j in range(n):
          A[j:j+n, j] = p
       A *= float(n)
       b=np.full(L, float(n), dtype=np.float64)
       c=-np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
       options={'presolve': True, 'time_limit': max(1, int(timeout))}
       try:
           res=sopt.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, None), method='highs', options=options)
       except Exception:
           res=sopt.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, None), options={'presolve': True})
       if res.status in (0,3) and res.x is not None:
           x=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
           x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
           x=np.clip(x, 0.0, None)
           s2=float(x.sum())
           if s2 > 1e-12:
              return x/s2
    except Exception:
       return None
    return None
```
Memory: A allocation in try. If MemoryError, returns None. Good.
Potential option `time_limit` as int; if timeout 20. If option invalid, fallback. If HiGHS not installed, method may error; fallback default method? In fallback, `method` omitted; might use default 'highs' anyway. If scipy older no HiGHS? Could use 'simplex' but dense 2048 slow. We can catch. If status >1, none.

Now, `refine_sequence` details. Let's write robust.

```
def refine_sequence(p0, rng, deadline, use_lp=False, max_lp=1000, lp_timeout=15.0):
    n=int(p0.size)
    p=floor_normalize(p0,1e-14)
    best_score=internal_score(p)
    best_p=p.copy()
    cur_p=p.copy()
    cur_score=best_score
    z=np.log(cur_p+1e-300); z -= float(np.mean(z))
    eta=0.05
    temps=(0.03,0.08,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0)
    temp_i=0
    fails=0
    it=0
    last_best=time.monotonic()
    last_lp=time.monotonic() - 100.0
    lp_count=0
    consider_candidate(cur_p, best_score)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
       it += 1
       now=time.monotonic()
       # reset if cur much worse than best and stagnant
       if now - last_best > 60.0 and cur_score > best_score + 1e-9:
           cur_p=best_p.copy(); cur_score=best_score
           z=np.log(cur_p+1e-300); z-=float(np.mean(z))
           eta=0.05; fails=0
       if it % 256 == 0:
           # floor
           p_temp=floor_normalize(cur_p,1e-14)
           if float(np.linalg.norm(p_temp - cur_p)) > 1e-12:
              cur_p=p_temp
              cur_score=internal_score(cur_p)
              z=np.log(cur_p+1e-14); z-=np.mean(z)
           best_p=floor_normalize(best_p,1e-14)  # don't recompute score? okay
       c=fft_convolve(cur_p, cur_p)
       c=np.where(c>0,c,0)
       maxc=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
       s=float(cur_p.sum())
       if s > 0 and maxc > 0 and np.isfinite(maxc):
          cur_score=float(2*n*maxc/(s*s))
       else:
          cur_score=np.inf
       if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
          cur_p=best_p.copy(); cur_score=best_score; z=...
          continue
       if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
          best_score=cur_score; best_p=cur_p.copy(); last_best=time.monotonic(); consider_candidate(best_p,best_score); fails=0
       improved=False
       if maxc > 0:
          temp=temps[temp_i % len(temps)]
          temp_i=(temp_i + 1 + int(rng.integers(0,2))) % len(temps)
          w=make_weights(c,temp)
          g=gradient_from_weights(cur_p,w)
          # sanitize g
          if g.size == n:
             g=np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
             mean_pg=float(np.dot(cur_p, g))
             grad = g - mean_pg
             gnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
             if np.isfinite(gnorm) and gnorm > 1e-15:
                # scale gradient to comparable? Maybe divide by (n?); but eta absorbs.
                # To prevent huge steps if p small, normalize grad by mean absolute? We can use eta as is. For p sum1, grad O(1). Good.
                for et in (eta, 0.5*eta):
                   if et <=0 or not np.isfinite(et): continue
                   z_new = z - et*grad
                   z_new = z_new - float(np.mean(z_new))
                   p_new, _ = log_softmax(z_new)
                   p_new = floor_normalize(p_new,1e-14)
                   sc=internal_score(p_new)
                   if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
                       z = z_new
                       # normalize z to log p maybe
                       z = np.log(p_new + 1e-300); z -= float(np.mean(z))
                       cur_p=p_new
                       cur_score=sc
                       if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                          best_score=sc; best_p=p_new.copy(); last_best=time.monotonic(); consider_candidate(best_p,best_score)
                       eta = min(max(et*1.1, 0.002), 1.5)
                       fails=0
                       improved=True
                       break
       if not improved:
          eta = max(eta*0.9, 0.001)
          fails += 1
          if fails > 15:
             got=False
             for scale in (0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0):
                z_r = random_perturb_z(z, rng, scale)
                z_r = z_r - float(np.mean(z_r))
                p_r, _ = log_softmax(z_r)
                p_r = floor_normalize(p_r,1e-14)
                sc_r=internal_score(p_r)
                if np.isfinite(sc_r) and (sc_r < cur_score - 1e-12):
                    z=np.log(p_r+1e-300); z-=np.mean(z)
                    cur_p=p_r; cur_score=sc_r
                    if sc_r < best_score - 1e-12:
                       best_score=sc_r; best_p=p_r.copy(); last_best=time.monotonic(); consider_candidate(best_p,best_score)
                    fails=0
                    got=True
                    break
             if not got and fails > 80:
                # try a small LP/random candidate? If use_lp false, reset
                cur_p=best_p.copy(); cur_score=best_score; z=...; fails=30
       # LP
       if use_lp and lp_count < max_lp:
          time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
          lp_interval = 25.0 if fails > 60 else 70.0
          if time.monotonic() - last_lp > lp_interval and time_left > lp_timeout + 30.0 and n <= 2048:
              base = best_p if best_score < cur_score else cur_p
              dirvec = lp_direction(base, timeout=lp_timeout)
              lp_count += 1
              last_lp = time.monotonic()
              if dirvec is not None:
                 best_mix_score=np.inf; best_mix_p=None
                 for t in (0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7,0.9,1.0):
                    p_m = (1.0-t)*base + t*dirvec
                    p_m = floor_normalize(p_m,1e-14)
                    sc_m=internal_score(p_m)
                    if np.isfinite(sc_m) and sc_m < best_mix_score:
                       best_mix_score=sc_m; best_mix_p=p_m
                 if best_mix_p is not None:
                    # accept if better than current or best, or mildly worse to explore
                    if (best_mix_score < cur_score - 1e-12) or (best_mix_score < best_score - 1e-12) or (best_mix_score < best_score + 0.02 and rng.random() < 0.5):
                       z=np.log(best_mix_p+1e-300); z-=np.mean(z)
                       cur_p=best_mix_p.copy(); cur_score=best_mix_score
                       eta=0.05; fails=0
                       if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                          best_score=cur_score; best_p=cur_p.copy(); last_best=time.monotonic(); consider_candidate(best_p,best_score)
    return best_p, best_score
```
Potential performance: `np.linalg.norm(p_temp-cur_p)` every 256 for 4096 ~ cheap. `internal_score` in floor recompute maybe okay. Could remove norm. But fine.

Potential bug: In `if np.isfinite(sc_r) and (sc_r < cur_score - 1e-12):` line parentheses okay.

`random_perturb_z` uses `rng.normal(0.0,scale,n)`. If scale integer, okay.

Now, `select_best_initial`:
```
def select_best_initial(n, rng, ref_seq=None):
    patterns=initial_patterns(n,rng,ref_seq)
    best_p=None; best_s=np.inf
    for p in patterns:
       p=floor_normalize(p,1e-14)
       if p.size != n: continue
       sc=internal_score(p)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s:
          best_s=sc; best_p=p
    if best_p is None:
       best_p=floor_normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),1e-14)
    return best_p
```

Now, `propose_candidate` detailed:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic()
    budget=float(budget_s)
    hard_deadline = start + max(60.0, budget - 20.0)
    total_work = max(60.0, min(budget - 120.0, 2300.0))
    _GLOBAL['best_score']=np.inf; _GLOBAL['best_seq']=None; _GLOBAL['top']=[]
    rng=np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 1234567)
    prev_seq=None
    prev_score=np.inf
    try:
       raw = normalize_sequence(height_sequence_1, 1.0)
       raw = floor_normalize(raw,1e-14)
       sc = exact_evaluate(raw)
       if not np.isfinite(sc): sc=internal_score(raw)
       prev_score=sc
       if np.isfinite(sc): consider_candidate(raw, sc)
       prev_seq=raw
       print("Starting construction score:", prev_score)
    except NameError:
       print("No height_sequence_1")
    except Exception as e:
       print(" previous read error", e)
    # stage deadlines based on start and total_work
    t1024 = start + 0.10*total_work
    t2048 = start + 0.45*total_work

    textra = start + total_work
    # maybe if budget small, adjust

    # Stage 1024

       n=1024
       p0=select_best_initial(n, rng, ref_seq)
       print("Stage n=1024 best initial", internal_score(p0))
       pb, sb = refine_sequence(p0, rng, min(t1024, hard_deadline), use_lp=True, max_lp=1000, lp_timeout=12.0)
       consider_candidate(pb, sb)
       if sb < _GLOBAL['best_score']: ref_seq = pb
       elif _GLOBAL['best_seq'] is not None: ref_seq = _GLOBAL['best_seq']
       print("Stage n=1024 end", sb)
    # Stage 2048
    if ref_seq is None and _GLOBAL['best_seq'] is not None: ref_seq = _GLOBAL['best_seq']
    stage_end = min(t2048, hard_deadline)
    if hard_deadline > stage_end + 30:
       p0=select_best_initial(2048, rng, ref_seq)
       pb, sb = refine_sequence(p0, rng, stage_end, use_lp=True, max_lp=1000, lp_timeout=20.0)
       ...
    # Stage 4096
    stage_end = min(t4096, hard_deadline)
    if hard_deadline > stage_end + 30:
       p0=select_best_initial(4096, rng, ref_seq)
       pb, sb = refine_sequence(p0, rng, stage_end, use_lp=False, max_lp=0, lp_timeout=0.0)
       ...
    # Extra

    if hard_deadline > extra_end + 30:
       if prev_seq is not None and 2048 <= prev_seq.size <= 5000:
          n=prev_seq.size
          use_lp = n <= 2048
          p0=select_best_initial(n, rng, ref_seq)
          # If p0 from ref_seq, maybe exact prev length ok

```python
"""
Search for low-self-convolution nonnegative step functions.

The objective is
    R(a) = 2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

The optimizer works on probability-like vectors summing to 1.  It combines:
  * several analytic/random monotone initializations, including inverse-like
    profiles that are known to beat the constant profile,
  * mirror-descent subgradient steps for the active convolution coefficients,
  * randomized low-pass/block restarts,
  * exact linear-programming (HiGHS) direction searches from the previous
    state-of-the-art LP idea, whenever memory/time permits.

The best vector found is verified with the provided evaluate_sequence().
"""

import numpy as np
import time

_GLOBAL = {"best_score": np.inf, "best_seq": None, "top": []}


def next_pow2(m):
    N = 1
    while N < m:
        N <<= 1
    return N


def fft_convolve(a, b=None):
    if b is None:
        b = a
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    m = b.size
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = n + m - 1
    N = next_pow2(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, N)
    return c[:L]


def internal_score(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.inf
    c = fft_convolve(x, x)
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(maxc):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * int(x.size) * maxc / (s * s))


def normalize_sequence(x, target_sum=1.0):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if x.size == 0 or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(max(1, int(x.size)), dtype=np.float64) * (
            target_sum / max(1, int(x.size))
        ) if x.size else np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    return x * (target_sum / s)


def floor_normalize(x, floor=1e-14):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size == 0:
        return x
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, None)
    if floor > 0.0:
        x = np.maximum(x, float(floor))
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.full_like(x, 1.0 / x.size)
    return x / s


def log_softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return z, z.copy()
    m = float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z - m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.full(z.size, 1.0 / z.size)
        out_z = np.zeros(z.size)
    else:
        p = e / s
        out_z = z - m - np.log(s)
    return p, out_z


def make_weights(c, temp):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.clip(c, 0.0, None)
    n = c.size
    if n == 0:
        return c
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    if maxc <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(maxc) or temp <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = (c - maxc) / (maxc * float(temp))
    x = x - float(np.max(x))
    w = np.exp(x)
    mx = float(np.max(w))
    if mx > 0.0 and np.isfinite(mx):
        w = w / mx
    else:
        w = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return w


def gradient_from_weights(p, w):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    conv = fft_convolve(p, w[::-1])
    if conv.size < 2 * n - 1:
        return zero.copy()
    return conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]


def random_perturb_z(z, rng, scale):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = z.size
    if n == 0:
        return z
    if scale <= 0.0:
        return z.copy()
    if rng.random() < 0.38:
        max_width = max(2, min(n, n // 6 + 2))
        min_width = 1
        if max_width > 1:
            width = int(rng.integers(min_width, max_width + 1))
        else:
            width = 1
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
        dr = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        dr[start:start + width] = rng.normal(0.0, float(scale), width)
    else:
        dr = rng.normal(0.0, float(scale), n)
        ker = np.ones(3, dtype=np.float64) / 3.0
        dr = np.convolve(dr, ker, mode="same")
        dr = np.convolve(dr, ker, mode="same")
    return z + dr


def resample_sequence(p, new_n):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    new_n = int(max(1, new_n))
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    p = p / s
    if n == new_n:
        return p

    old = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) - 0.5) / float(n)
    new = (np.arange(new_n, dtype=np.float64) - 0.5) / float(new_n)
    logs = np.log(p + 1e-300)
    new_logs = np.interp(new, old, logs)
    q = np.exp(new_logs - float(np.max(new_logs)))
    sq = float(q.sum())
    if sq <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sq):
        q = np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    else:
        q = q / sq
    return q


def initial_patterns(n, rng, ref_seq=None):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    pats = []
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)

    pats.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    for off in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        pats.append(np.power(x + off, -0.5))

    for alpha in (0.45, 0.48, 0.5, 0.52, 0.55, 0.60):
        left = np.power(x + 1.0, -alpha)
        for beta in (0.0, 0.2, 0.5, 0.8):
            if beta == 0.0:
                p = left
            else:
                right = np.maximum(n - x, 0.5)
                p = left * np.power(right, -beta)
            pats.append(p)

    for w in (0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        p = np.power(x + 0.5, -0.5) + w * np.power(n + 0.5 - x, -0.5)
        pats.append(p)

    n_random = 4 if n <= 2048 else (2 if n <= 4096 else 1)
    for _ in range(n_random):
        y = rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, n)
        ker = np.ones(3, dtype=np.float64) / 3.0
        y = np.convolve(y, ker, mode="same")
        y = np.convolve(y, ker, mode="same")
        y -= float(np.mean(y))
        pats.append(np.exp(y - float(np.max(y))))

    inv = np.power(x + 0.5, -0.5)
    for _ in range(3 if n <= 4096 else 1):
        pats.append(rng.permutation(inv))

    if ref_seq is not None:
        ref = np.asarray(ref_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        s = float(ref.sum())
        if ref.size > 0 and np.isfinite(ref).all() and s > 0.0:
            ref = ref / s
            pats.append(ref.copy() if ref.size == n else resample_sequence(ref, n))

    return pats


def select_best_initial(n, rng, ref_seq=None):
    pats = initial_patterns(n, rng, ref_seq)
    best_p = None
    best_s = np.inf
    for p in pats:
        p = floor_normalize(p, 1e-14)
        if p.size != int(n):
            continue
        sc = internal_score(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s:
            best_s = sc
            best_p = p
    if best_p is None:
        best_p = floor_normalize(np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64), 1e-14)
    return best_p


def lp_direction(p, timeout=15.0):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 16 or timeout <= 0.0:
        return None
    try:
        import scipy.optimize as sopt
    except Exception:
        return None

    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    p = floor_normalize(p, 1e-8)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    try:
        A = np.zeros((L, n), dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(n):
            A[j:j + n, j] = p
        A *= float(n)
        b = np.full(L, float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

        options = {"presolve": True, "time_limit": max(1, int(timeout))}
        try:
            res = sopt.linprog(
                c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds,
                method="highs", options=options
            )
        except Exception:
            options = {"presolve": True}
            res = sopt.linprog(
                c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds,
                method="highs", options=options
            )

        if res.status in (0, 3) and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
            x = np.clip(x, 0.0, None)
            s2 = float(x.sum())
            if s2 > 1e-12:
                return x / s2
    except Exception:
        return None
    return None


def consider_candidate(seq, score):
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if seq.size == 0:
        return
    seq = np.where(np.isfinite(seq), seq, 0.0)
    seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(seq.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return
    nseq = seq / s

    if not np.isfinite(score):
        score = internal_score(nseq)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    score = float(score)

    if score < _GLOBAL["best_score"] - 1e-12:
        _GLOBAL["best_score"] = score
        _GLOBAL["best_seq"] = nseq.copy()

    for entry in _GLOBAL["top"]:
        if entry[0].size == nseq.size and np.linalg.norm(entry[0] - nseq) < 1e-8:
            return
    else:
        if len(_GLOBAL["top"]) >= 60:
            worst = 0
            for i in range(1, len(_GLOBAL["top"])):
                if _GLOBAL["top"][i][1] > _GLOBAL["top"][worst][1]:
                    worst = i
            if score >= _GLOBAL["top"][worst][1]:
                return
            del _GLOBAL["top"][worst]
        _GLOBAL["top"].append((nseq.copy(), score))


def refine_sequence(p0, rng, deadline, use_lp=False, max_lp=1000, lp_timeout=15.0):
    n = int(p0.size)
    p = floor_normalize(p0, 1e-14)

    best_score = internal_score(p)
    best_p = p.copy()
    cur_p = p.copy()
    cur_score = best_score

    z = np.log(cur_p + 1e-300)
    z -= float(np.mean(z))

    eta = 0.10
    temps = (0.03, 0.08, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0)
    temp_i = 0
    fails = 0
    it = 0
    last_best = time.monotonic()
    last_lp = time.monotonic() - 1000.0
    lp_count = 0

    consider_candidate(cur_p, best_score)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        it += 1
        now = time.monotonic()

        if now - last_best > 60.0 and cur_score > best_score + 1e-9:
            cur_p = best_p.copy()
            cur_score = best_score
            z = np.log(cur_p + 1e-300)
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            eta = 0.10
            fails = 0

        if it % 256 == 0:
            cur_p = floor_normalize(cur_p, 1e-14)
            cur_score = internal_score(cur_p)
            z = np.log(cur_p + 1e-300)
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            best_p = floor_normalize(best_p, 1e-14)

        c = fft_convolve(cur_p, cur_p)
        c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
        c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
        maxc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        s = float(cur_p.sum())
        if s > 0.0 and maxc > 0.0 and np.isfinite(maxc):
            cur_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc / (s * s))
        else:
            cur_score = np.inf

        if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
            cur_p = best_p.copy()
            cur_score = best_score
            z = np.log(cur_p + 1e-300)
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            continue

        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_p = cur_p.copy()
            last_best = time.monotonic()
            consider_candidate(best_p, best_score)
            fails = 0

        improved = False
        if maxc > 0.0:
            temp = temps[temp_i % len(temps)]
            temp_i = (temp_i + 1 + int(rng.integers(0, 2))) % len(temps)
            w = make_weights(c, temp)
            g = gradient_from_weights(cur_p, w)
            if g.size == n:
                g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
                mean_pg = float(np.dot(cur_p, g))
                grad = g - mean_pg
                gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
                if np.isfinite(gnorm) and gnorm > 1e-15:
                    for et in (eta, 0.5 * eta):
                        if et <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(et):
                            continue
                        z_new = z - et * grad
                        z_new -= float(np.mean(z_new))
                        p_new, _ = log_softmax(z_new)
                        p_new = floor_normalize(p_new, 1e-14)
                        sc = internal_score(p_new)
                        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
                            z = np.log(p_new + 1e-300)
                            z -= float(np.mean(z))
                            cur_p = p_new
                            cur_score = sc
                            if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                                best_score = sc
                                best_p = p_new.copy()
                                last_best = time.monotonic()
                                consider_candidate(best_p, best_score)
                            eta = min(max(et * 1.10, 0.002), 0.5)
                            fails = 0
                            improved = True
                            break

        if not improved:
            eta = max(eta * 0.90, 0.001)
            fails += 1
            if fails > 15:
                got = False
                for scale in (0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0):
                    z_r = random_perturb_z(z, rng, scale)
                    z_r -= float(np.mean(z_r))
                    p_r, _ = log_softmax(z_r)
                    p_r = floor_normalize(p_r, 1e-14)
                    sc_r = internal_score(p_r)
                    if np.isfinite(sc_r) and sc_r < cur_score - 1e-12:
                        z = np.log(p_r + 1e-300)
                        z -= float(np.mean(z))
                        cur_p = p_r
                        cur_score = sc_r
                        if sc_r < best_score - 1e-12:
                            best_score = sc_r
                            best_p = p_r.copy()
                            last_best = time.monotonic()
                            consider_candidate(best_p, best_score)
                        fails = 0
                        got = True
                        break
                if not got and fails > 80:
                    cur_p = best_p.copy()
                    cur_score = best_score
                    z = np.log(cur_p + 1e-300)
                    z -= float(np.mean(z))
                    fails = 30

        if use_lp and lp_count < max_lp and n <= 2048:
            time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
            lp_interval = 25.0 if fails > 60 else 55.0
            if (
                time.monotonic() - last_lp > lp_interval
                and time_left > float(lp_timeout) + 30.0
            ):
                base = best_p if best_score < cur_score else cur_p
                dirvec = lp_direction(base, timeout=lp_timeout)
                lp_count += 1
                last_lp = time.monotonic()
                if dirvec is not None:
                    best_mix_score = np.inf
                    best_mix_p = None
                    for t in (0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.7, 0.9, 1.0):
                        p_m = (1.0 - t) * base + t * dirvec
                        p_m = floor_normalize(p_m, 1e-14)
                        sc_m = internal_score(p_m)
                        if np.isfinite(sc_m) and sc_m < best_mix_score:
                            best_mix_score = sc_m
                            best_mix_p = p_m
                    if best_mix_p is not None:
                        accept = (
                            best_mix_score < cur_score - 1e-12
                            or best_mix_score < best_score - 1e-12
                            or (best_mix_score < best_score + 0.02 and rng.random() < 0.5)
                        )
                        if accept:
                            z = np.log(best_mix_p + 1e-300)
                            z -= float(np.mean(z))
                            cur_p = best_mix_p.copy()
                            cur_score = best_mix_score
                            eta = 0.10
                            fails = 0
                            if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                                best_score = cur_score
                                best_p = cur_p.copy()
                                last_best = time.monotonic()
                                consider_candidate(best_p, best_score)

    return best_p, best_score


def exact_evaluate(seq):
    try:
        return float(evaluate_sequence(seq.tolist()))
    except Exception:
        return np.inf


def is_duplicate(cands, cand):
    for c in cands:
        if c is not None and c.size == cand.size and np.linalg.norm(c - cand) < 1e-8:
            return True
    return False


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Search for the best valid sequence within the allotted time budget."""
    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(budget_s if budget_s is not None else 2400.0)
    hard_deadline = start + max(120.0, budget - 30.0)
    total_work = max(90.0, min(budget - 120.0, 2280.0))

    _GLOBAL["best_score"] = np.inf
    _GLOBAL["best_seq"] = None
    _GLOBAL["top"] = []

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 123456789)

    prev_seq = None
    try:
        prev = normalize_sequence(height_sequence_1, 1.0)
        prev = floor_normalize(prev, 1e-14)
        if prev.size > 0:
            sc = exact_evaluate(prev)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                sc = internal_score(prev)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                consider_candidate(prev, sc)
            prev_seq = prev
            print("Existing construction score: %.10f" % sc)
    except Exception:
        print("No usable height_sequence_1.")

    ref_seq = prev_seq
    if ref_seq is None and _GLOBAL["best_seq"] is not None:
        ref_seq = _GLOBAL["best_seq"]

    def run_stage(n, stage_deadline, use_lp, lp_to):
        nonlocal ref_seq
        stage_deadline = min(float(stage_deadline), hard_deadline)
        if hard_deadline <= stage_deadline + 25.0:
            return
        n = int(max(2, n))
        if ref_seq is not None:
            p0 = select_best_initial(n, rng, ref_seq)
        else:
            p0 = select_best_initial(n, rng, None)
        init_score = internal_score(p0)
        print("Stage n=%d starting, initial internal score %.10f" % (n, init_score))
        pb, sb = refine_sequence(
            p0, rng, stage_deadline,
            use_lp=use_lp,
            max_lp=1000,
            lp_timeout=lp_to
        )
        consider_candidate(pb, sb)
        if sb < _GLOBAL["best_score"]:
            ref_seq = pb
        elif _GLOBAL["best_seq"] is not None:
            ref_seq = _GLOBAL["best_seq"]
        print("Stage n=%d finished, internal score %.10f" % (n, sb))

    t1 = start + 0.10 * total_work
    t2 = start + 0.45 * total_work
    t3 = start + 0.85 * total_work
    t4 = start + total_work

    run_stage(1024, t1, True, 12.0)
    run_stage(2048, t2, True, 20.0)
    run_stage(4096, t3, False, 0.0)

    extra_end = min(t4, hard_deadline)
    if hard_deadline > extra_end + 30.0:
        if prev_seq is not None and 2048 <= prev_seq.size <= 5000:
            n = prev_seq.size
            use_lp = n <= 2048
            p0 = select_best_initial(n, rng, ref_seq)
            pb, sb = refine_sequence(
                p0, rng, extra_end,
                use_lp=use_lp,
                max_lp=1000,
                lp_timeout=18.0
            )
            consider_candidate(pb, sb)
            if sb < _GLOBAL["best_score"]:
                ref_seq = pb
        elif hard_deadline - time.monotonic() > 260.0:
            p0 = select_best_initial(8192, rng, ref_seq)
            pb, sb = refine_sequence(
                p0, rng, extra_end,
                use_lp=False,
                max_lp=0,
                lp_timeout=0.0
            )
            consider_candidate(pb, sb)

    # Final exact verification and selection.
    final_candidates = []
    if _GLOBAL["best_seq"] is not None:
        final_candidates.append(_GLOBAL["best_seq"].copy())
    if prev_seq is not None:
        final_candidates.append(prev_seq.copy())

    ordered = []
    for entry in _GLOBAL["top"]:
        score = entry[1]
        pos = len(ordered)
        for i in range(len(ordered)):
            if score < ordered[i][1]:
                pos = i
                break
        ordered.insert(pos, entry)
        if len(ordered) > 40:
            ordered.pop()

    for entry in ordered:
        if len(final_candidates) >= 22:
            break
        if not is_duplicate(final_candidates, entry[0]):
            final_candidates.append(entry[0].copy())

    final_best_score = np.inf
    final_seq = None
    for cand in final_candidates:
        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 2.0:
            break
        try:
            sc = exact_evaluate(cand)
        except Exception:
            sc = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_best_score:
            final_best_score = sc
            final_seq = cand.copy()

    if final_seq is None:
        final_seq = _GLOBAL["best_seq"] if _GLOBAL["best_seq"] is not None else np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        print("Final exact best score: %.12f" % final_best_score)

    final_seq = np.asarray(final_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    final_seq = np.where(np.isfinite(final_seq), final_seq, 0.0)
    final_seq = np.clip(final_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(final_seq.sum())
    if final_seq.size == 0 or s < 0.01:
        final_seq = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
        return [float(v) for v in final_seq]

    # Return as a probability vector; scaling is invariant under the evaluator
    # as long as values remain below the 1000 cap and the sum is >= 0.01.
    final_seq = final_seq / s
    return [float(v) for v in final_seq]
```
